Data, Review, View, Automate, Help.

55
00:01:35.950 --> 00:01:36.230
Do you see this?

56
00:01:36.630 --> 00:01:37.890
We call it Toolbar.

57
00:01:38.390 --> 00:01:42.730
And what you see here, this box is

58
00:01:42.730 --> 00:01:43.730
called Formula Bar.

59
00:01:44.250 --> 00:01:46.030
I will write a formula here and show

60
00:01:46.030 --> 00:01:46.190
you.

61
00:01:46.290 --> 00:01:47.510
But don't get confused.

62
00:01:47.510 --> 00:01:48.770
In the coming videos, I will teach you

63
00:01:48.770 --> 00:01:50.230
everything from scratch.

64
00:01:50.690 --> 00:01:51.830
If I write here equal to.

65
00:01:52.230 --> 00:01:55.990
And then I write here 9 plus 6.

66
00:01:56.210 --> 00:01:57.210
And if I hit enter, it will be

67
00:01:57.210 --> 00:01:58.270
automatically calculated.

68
00:01:58.570 --> 00:01:59.490
Because this is a formula bar.

69
00:01:59.610 --> 00:02:00.250
And here we can write the formula of

70
00:02:00.250 --> 00:02:00.770
any cell.

71
00:02:02.290 --> 00:02:03.390
I will also empty it by pressing backspace.

72
00:02:04.530 --> 00:02:07.770
Whenever you open the Excel workbook, you are

73
00:02:07.770 --> 00:02:08.509
in the Home tab by default.

74
00:02:09.210 --> 00:02:12.250
And in the Home tab, you can see

75
00:02:12.250 --> 00:02:14.270
cut, copy, format printer.

76
00:02:14.570 --> 00:02:15.730
Which font are you using?

77
00:02:16.070 --> 00:02:16.990
What is the font size?

78
00:02:16.990 --> 00:02:18.370
Bold, Italic, Underline.

79
00:02:18.490 --> 00:02:19.070
You can see all these things.

80
00:02:20.250 --> 00:02:24.930
I can safely say that the most important

81
00:02:24.930 --> 00:02:26.090
commands are in the Home tab.

82
00:02:26.450 --> 00:02:27.170
And that's why it is kept on the

83
00:02:27.170 --> 00:02:27.570
Home tab.

84
00:02:28.170 --> 00:02:30.470
Because there are some things that you will

85
00:02:30.470 --> 00:02:30.930
use in Excel.

86
00:02:31.250 --> 00:02:34.330
For example, you can want to centre your

87
00:02:34.330 --> 00:02:34.430
text.

88
00:02:34.890 --> 00:02:37.750
You can want to centre the alignment left

89
00:02:37.750 --> 00:02:38.870
or right.

90
00:02:39.810 --> 00:02:41.630
You can want to apply conditional formatting.

91
00:02:42.310 --> 00:02:43.210
About which we will talk later.

92
00:02:44.030 --> 00:02:44.530
You can want to do autosum.

93
00:02:44.530 --> 00:02:46.650
That is, you can automatically sum the elements

94
00:02:46.650 --> 00:02:47.850
of a particular column.

95
00:02:49.530 --> 00:02:50.990
You can want to sort and filter.

96
00:02:51.750 --> 00:02:55.050
Because filtering is very common in data.

97
00:02:55.590 --> 00:02:58.150
And this is distributed in cells, which is

98
00:02:58.150 --> 00:02:58.710
your data.

99
00:02:58.830 --> 00:02:59.950
So that you can do filtering on the

100
00:02:59.950 --> 00:03:01.150
basis of rows and columns.

101
00:03:02.650 --> 00:03:04.450
And according to the filter, you will be

102
00:03:04.450 --> 00:03:05.150
able to see what is happening in which

103
00:03:05.150 --> 00:03:05.450
data.

104
00:03:06.570 --> 00:03:07.230
Why is it happening?

105
00:03:07.530 --> 00:03:08.830
So all these things are given to you

106
00:03:08.830 --> 00:03:09.510
in the Home tab.

107
00:03:10.150 --> 00:03:11.370
Which you will use the most.

108
00:03:12.550 --> 00:03:14.190
After this comes the Insert tab.

109
00:03:14.290 --> 00:03:15.130
You have to insert a table.

110
00:03:15.810 --> 00:03:16.570
Maybe you have to insert a picture.

111
00:03:17.290 --> 00:03:18.350
Maybe you have to insert an online picture.

112
00:03:19.130 --> 00:03:19.630
You have to insert a chart.

113
00:03:20.410 --> 00:03:21.690
You do that with the Insert tab.

114
00:03:22.930 --> 00:03:25.110
After this, the third tab is the Draw

115
00:03:25.110 --> 00:03:25.390
tab.

116
00:03:26.070 --> 00:03:27.830
And there is a good chance that you

117
00:03:27.830 --> 00:03:29.210
do not see this in your version of

118
00:03:29.210 --> 00:03:29.850
Microsoft Excel.

119
00:03:31.370 --> 00:03:33.250
The reason for this may be that the

120
00:03:33.250 --> 00:03:35.950
Draw tab does not show in some versions.

121
00:03:37.050 --> 00:03:37.870
It does not show in some devices.

122
00:03:38.790 --> 00:03:39.970
And you do not have to worry at

123
00:03:39.970 --> 00:03:40.070
all.

124
00:03:40.510 --> 00:03:42.030
The only work of the Draw tab is

125
00:03:42.030 --> 00:03:44.330
that you can select a pen and draw

126
00:03:44.330 --> 00:03:44.830
here.

127
00:03:45.510 --> 00:03:49.010
And to be very honest, this thing is

128
00:03:49.010 --> 00:03:49.870
not that important in Excel.

129
00:03:50.330 --> 00:03:51.530
If I tell you, then you can click

130
00:03:51.530 --> 00:03:53.550
on this arrow and select this ink and

131
00:03:53.550 --> 00:03:54.330
delete it.

132
00:03:54.830 --> 00:03:57.830
But again, because in other Microsoft products, the

133
00:03:57.830 --> 00:03:58.610
Draw tab is given.

134
00:03:59.350 --> 00:04:00.570
They have also included it in Excel.

135
00:04:01.130 --> 00:04:03.110
To be very very honest, the Draw tab

136
00:04:03.110 --> 00:04:03.750
does not work that much.

137
00:04:04.290 --> 00:04:05.730
But again, I personally use it.

138
00:04:05.910 --> 00:04:07.190
I will show you when and how.

139
00:04:07.190 --> 00:04:09.010
A very good use case is to put

140
00:04:09.010 --> 00:04:09.410
a signature.

141
00:04:09.930 --> 00:04:11.030
Sometimes I have to put a signature.

142
00:04:11.930 --> 00:04:13.350
And then I have to take the printout

143
00:04:13.350 --> 00:04:13.570
after that.

144
00:04:13.810 --> 00:04:14.930
And my data is in Excel.

145
00:04:15.490 --> 00:04:16.610
So the Draw tab works a lot.

146
00:04:17.370 --> 00:04:20.190
But again, to manipulate data, the Draw tab

147
00:04:20.190 --> 00:04:20.690
does not work that much.

148
00:04:21.290 --> 00:04:23.070
So if your Draw tab is not visible,

149
00:04:23.290 --> 00:04:24.350
it is totally fine.

150
00:04:24.950 --> 00:04:26.210
Draw tab works at one place.

151
00:04:27.090 --> 00:04:30.190
And it comes when you use an iPad.

152
00:04:30.730 --> 00:04:32.070
Or a Surface tab.

153
00:04:32.270 --> 00:04:36.170
Which is a Windows computer from Microsoft.

154
00:04:36.170 --> 00:04:38.850
And you can use this tab in it.

155
00:04:39.430 --> 00:04:40.870
And you can do drawing etc.

156
00:04:42.390 --> 00:04:43.250
After this, page layout.

157
00:04:43.730 --> 00:04:45.870
How to see margins, how to see colours,

158
00:04:46.010 --> 00:04:46.630
what is the width and height.

159
00:04:47.310 --> 00:04:47.970
You can select all this from the page

160
00:04:47.970 --> 00:04:48.270
layout.

161
00:04:50.130 --> 00:04:50.930
Then formulas.

162
00:04:51.390 --> 00:04:52.730
Financial formulas.

163
00:04:53.590 --> 00:04:54.150
Autosum.

164
00:04:54.610 --> 00:04:56.330
How to format date and time.

165
00:04:57.050 --> 00:04:58.810
All these things are in your formulas tab.

166
00:04:59.750 --> 00:05:01.470
After this, the data tab is very important.

167
00:05:01.750 --> 00:05:03.310
From where you can import data.

168
00:05:03.870 --> 00:05:04.470
You can remove duplicates.

169
00:05:04.470 --> 00:05:06.150
You can do text to columns.

170
00:05:06.610 --> 00:05:07.730
About which I will tell you.

171
00:05:08.330 --> 00:05:10.690
And along with that, you can do forecasting.

172
00:05:11.950 --> 00:05:12.950
After this, there is a review tab.

173
00:05:13.570 --> 00:05:15.250
In which you can see spell checks and

174
00:05:15.250 --> 00:05:17.510
things related to reviewing your documents.

175
00:05:18.970 --> 00:05:20.590
After this, in view, you get options like

176
00:05:20.590 --> 00:05:22.450
page break, remove, etc.

177
00:05:23.490 --> 00:05:25.130
You can freeze the pins.

178
00:05:25.690 --> 00:05:28.090
And what does it mean, we will see

179
00:05:28.090 --> 00:05:28.330
about it later.

180
00:05:29.010 --> 00:05:30.890
In Automate, you can write scripts.

181
00:05:31.590 --> 00:05:33.810
And by writing scripts, you can automate your

182
00:05:33.810 --> 00:05:35.370
Excel manual tasks.

183
00:05:36.750 --> 00:05:37.830
After this, there is a help tab.

184
00:05:38.630 --> 00:05:41.150
Which is not very useful to be honest.

185
00:05:41.310 --> 00:05:42.930
Its name is help, but I will be

186
00:05:42.930 --> 00:05:43.610
very honest with you.

187
00:05:43.670 --> 00:05:45.030
I personally don't use it at all.

188
00:05:45.270 --> 00:05:46.650
Because all the help I am going to

189
00:05:46.650 --> 00:05:47.670
give you in this video.

190
00:05:47.910 --> 00:05:48.990
So you guys don't worry at all.

191
00:05:49.830 --> 00:05:50.990
Now here I will tell you a very

192
00:05:50.990 --> 00:05:51.650
good tip.

193
00:05:52.310 --> 00:05:53.270
Suppose you are at home.

194
00:05:53.730 --> 00:05:56.230
Now you feel that my computer screen is

195
00:05:56.230 --> 00:05:56.910
already very small.

196
00:05:57.070 --> 00:05:57.750
Mine is not small.

197
00:05:58.530 --> 00:05:59.890
Your computer screen can be small.

198
00:06:00.750 --> 00:06:01.930
If you guys are small.

199
00:06:02.650 --> 00:06:04.250
Then you guys can double click on home.

200
00:06:04.990 --> 00:06:06.310
You can hide this particular ribbon.

201
00:06:08.350 --> 00:06:09.450
Now it is gone, how to bring it

202
00:06:09.450 --> 00:06:09.750
back?

203
00:06:10.130 --> 00:06:12.190
You can click anywhere and bring it back.

204
00:06:12.510 --> 00:06:13.870
And if you click outside, it will go.

205
00:06:14.750 --> 00:06:16.450
But you can say that I want to

206
00:06:16.450 --> 00:06:17.670
revert to the previous configuration.

207
00:06:19.490 --> 00:06:20.910
So you guys can double click like this.

208
00:06:21.650 --> 00:06:22.970
Double click on home like this.

209
00:06:23.410 --> 00:06:25.170
And automatically you guys came to the same

210
00:06:25.170 --> 00:06:26.350
configuration in which you were.

211
00:06:26.730 --> 00:06:27.850
I use a big monitor.

212
00:06:28.570 --> 00:06:30.950
And personally my preference is that I will

213
00:06:30.950 --> 00:06:31.570
keep it like this.

214
00:06:31.810 --> 00:06:33.090
I want that when I am working.

215
00:06:33.270 --> 00:06:33.950
Then at least I can see the content

216
00:06:33.950 --> 00:06:34.730
inside the home tab.

217
00:06:36.450 --> 00:06:37.970
So if you guys want this to happen,

218
00:06:38.010 --> 00:06:38.970
then keep it like this.

219
00:06:38.990 --> 00:06:40.150
But if you guys are working on a

220
00:06:40.150 --> 00:06:40.550
laptop.

221
00:06:41.170 --> 00:06:43.310
Where space is a very big constraint for

222
00:06:43.310 --> 00:06:43.510
you.

223
00:06:44.290 --> 00:06:45.670
Then you guys can double click on it.

224
00:06:45.910 --> 00:06:46.630
And you will get a lot of space

225
00:06:46.630 --> 00:06:47.370
to work with your data.

226
00:06:48.310 --> 00:06:49.810
Or you guys keep it like this.

227
00:06:49.870 --> 00:06:50.690
If your computer is small.

228
00:06:51.090 --> 00:06:52.390
And whenever you feel.

229
00:06:53.370 --> 00:06:55.390
Yes, now I am working with home.

230
00:06:55.390 --> 00:06:58.110
So double click and expand it.

231
00:06:58.170 --> 00:06:58.730
It's up to you.

232
00:06:59.010 --> 00:07:00.370
You can expand whenever you want by double

233
00:07:00.370 --> 00:07:00.610
clicking.

234
00:07:01.270 --> 00:07:02.270
You can contract whenever you want.

235
00:07:02.710 --> 00:07:03.990
This was a very important tip.

236
00:07:04.410 --> 00:07:06.230
After this, there is a quick access toolbar

237
00:07:06.230 --> 00:07:06.390
here.

238
00:07:06.570 --> 00:07:08.250
Like you see here, if I hover it.

239
00:07:08.350 --> 00:07:09.910
So it is undoing and redoing.

240
00:07:09.950 --> 00:07:11.850
Now if you click here on this button.

241
00:07:12.450 --> 00:07:13.630
So you guys will get options to remove

242
00:07:13.630 --> 00:07:15.010
undo and redo here.

243
00:07:15.330 --> 00:07:17.090
See if you click here and click on

244
00:07:17.090 --> 00:07:17.570
undo here.

245
00:07:17.990 --> 00:07:18.770
So this undo is removed.

246
00:07:19.170 --> 00:07:20.330
Click here you guys.

247
00:07:20.790 --> 00:07:21.690
You can bring undo again.

248
00:07:22.090 --> 00:07:24.390
So basically you have to click on this.

249
00:07:24.390 --> 00:07:25.450
And after clicking on this toolbar.

250
00:07:26.390 --> 00:07:27.570
You guys can bring undo and redo.

251
00:07:29.710 --> 00:07:31.050
Suppose I wrote something here.

252
00:07:31.710 --> 00:07:33.150
Suppose I deleted it.

253
00:07:33.650 --> 00:07:35.590
So I can undo it by clicking here.

254
00:07:35.690 --> 00:07:36.830
So sometimes you guys will want.

255
00:07:37.050 --> 00:07:38.730
That you can undo and redo again.

256
00:07:39.010 --> 00:07:40.310
This is redo, this is undo, this is

257
00:07:40.310 --> 00:07:40.590
redo.

258
00:07:41.050 --> 00:07:42.090
So what happens by undo and redo.

259
00:07:42.570 --> 00:07:43.430
That the work you have done.

260
00:07:43.510 --> 00:07:44.270
It should be done again.

261
00:07:44.570 --> 00:07:45.930
And if you have done undo by mistake.

262
00:07:46.230 --> 00:07:47.070
Then you can redo it.

263
00:07:47.810 --> 00:07:49.870
You can undo it again.

264
00:07:49.990 --> 00:07:50.850
That means you can undo the undo too.

265
00:07:51.310 --> 00:07:52.550
So here undo means.

266
00:07:52.550 --> 00:07:53.830
Do means to work.

267
00:07:54.270 --> 00:07:56.110
Undo means that the work I have done.

268
00:07:56.470 --> 00:07:57.130
Revert it.

269
00:07:57.210 --> 00:07:57.770
It should not be.

270
00:07:58.030 --> 00:08:01.030
Suppose you wrote something here.

271
00:08:01.790 --> 00:08:03.310
Now you think you wrote it by mistake.

272
00:08:03.490 --> 00:08:03.990
Now undo it.

273
00:08:04.090 --> 00:08:04.410
It will be removed.

274
00:08:04.830 --> 00:08:06.270
Or suppose I wrote something good here.

275
00:08:06.490 --> 00:08:07.410
I wrote here.

276
00:08:07.930 --> 00:08:10.690
The cat sat on the mat.

277
00:08:10.910 --> 00:08:11.070
Okay.

278
00:08:12.470 --> 00:08:14.650
Now suppose someone set the sat by mistake.

279
00:08:15.830 --> 00:08:18.010
And here I wrote sitting by mistake.

280
00:08:18.630 --> 00:08:19.250
Now you say to him.

281
00:08:19.370 --> 00:08:19.830
What did you do?

282
00:08:19.910 --> 00:08:20.470
Why did you spoil my sentence?

283
00:08:20.470 --> 00:08:22.690
He will say, undo it.

284
00:08:22.850 --> 00:08:23.470
If you undo it.

285
00:08:23.650 --> 00:08:25.010
Then you will go to the previous configuration.

286
00:08:26.430 --> 00:08:26.810
Now assume.

287
00:08:27.450 --> 00:08:28.990
He has to tell someone that he did

288
00:08:28.990 --> 00:08:29.270
it wrong.

289
00:08:29.510 --> 00:08:30.270
But what did he do wrong?

290
00:08:30.390 --> 00:08:30.930
What did he write?

291
00:08:31.450 --> 00:08:32.030
Try redoing it.

292
00:08:32.929 --> 00:08:33.330
What was written?

293
00:08:33.730 --> 00:08:34.350
Undo it again.

294
00:08:35.010 --> 00:08:35.169
Okay.

295
00:08:35.289 --> 00:08:35.870
So undo is done by redo.

296
00:08:36.350 --> 00:08:36.530
Okay.

297
00:08:36.789 --> 00:08:38.150
As you work with excel.

298
00:08:38.390 --> 00:08:39.150
You will understand.

299
00:08:39.309 --> 00:08:40.090
What happens by undo and redo.

300
00:08:40.830 --> 00:08:41.070
Okay.

301
00:08:41.610 --> 00:08:42.490
So in this way.

302
00:08:42.789 --> 00:08:45.470
You can customise your quick access toolbar here.

303
00:08:46.350 --> 00:08:47.810
Now suppose I don't want to see the

304
00:08:47.810 --> 00:08:47.910
save.

305
00:08:47.910 --> 00:08:48.790
I don't want to see this save.

306
00:08:49.090 --> 00:08:49.810
So I can remove it from here.

307
00:08:50.230 --> 00:08:51.030
Suppose I don't even want to see undo

308
00:08:51.030 --> 00:08:51.210
and redo.

309
00:08:51.750 --> 00:08:52.670
I don't want to click undo and redo

310
00:08:52.670 --> 00:08:53.250
here.

311
00:08:53.290 --> 00:08:53.850
So I can remove it.

312
00:08:54.670 --> 00:08:56.710
I have a lot of options here.

313
00:08:56.770 --> 00:08:57.050
You can see them.

314
00:08:57.270 --> 00:08:58.890
Suppose I want to see the print preview

315
00:08:58.890 --> 00:08:59.270
here.

316
00:08:59.490 --> 00:09:00.330
So I will check it.

317
00:09:00.670 --> 00:09:01.610
And I will get to see the print

318
00:09:01.610 --> 00:09:03.430
icon here.

319
00:09:03.730 --> 00:09:04.750
Then I can click on it and see

320
00:09:04.750 --> 00:09:05.470
the print preview.

321
00:09:05.890 --> 00:09:07.830
That is, how will my sheet look while

322
00:09:07.830 --> 00:09:07.930

323
00:09:08.470 --> 00:09:09.570
And I can also print.

324
00:09:09.670 --> 00:09:11.850
I have Microsoft Print to PDF Printer here.

325
00:09:11.970 --> 00:09:13.170
Which prints it in PDF.

326
00:09:14.310 --> 00:09:15.290
We will talk about it later.

327
00:09:15.850 --> 00:09:17.850
But you can customise it.

328
00:09:18.710 --> 00:09:19.750
Sort ascending.

329
00:09:20.070 --> 00:09:21.010
You can bring it here.

330
00:09:21.870 --> 00:09:23.350
And there are a lot of things that

331
00:09:23.350 --> 00:09:24.390
you want to see at the top.

332
00:09:24.770 --> 00:09:26.330
And you can keep them.

333
00:09:26.530 --> 00:09:27.370
But what will I do now?

334
00:09:27.830 --> 00:09:28.410
That I will keep undo and redo.

335
00:09:28.930 --> 00:09:32.350
Because according to me, it feels good to

336
00:09:32.350 --> 00:09:32.450
be on top of undo and redo.

337
00:09:32.530 --> 00:09:32.630
Okay.

00:09:46.190 --> 00:09:46.450

00:09:56.530 --> 00:09:57.850

358

359

I am not saying that you also have

386
00:10:51.030 --> 00:10:51.710

But I prefer it personally.

388
00:10:54.790 --> 00:10:56.530
So that I can zoom anywhere like this.

389
00:10:57.350 --> 00:10:58.690
I can zoom like this.

390
00:10:58.890 --> 00:10:59.070

00:10:59.550 --> 00:11:01.190
So by clicking here, this work can be

392
00:11:01.190 --> 00:11:01.550

396
00:11:07.130 --> 00:11:08.750
After this, you get the option to see

00:11:10.650 --> 00:11:11.990
If you want to see like this.

00:11:13.390 --> 00:11:14.010
You can click here.

You can zoom in and zoom out.

402
00:11:16.210 --> 00:11:16.830
But again.

00:11:17.790 --> 00:11:18.610

404

407
00:11:26.170 --> 00:11:27.930
Keep it optimised for best appearance.

00:11:29.730 --> 00:11:30.630

WEBVTT

1
00:00:00.140 --> 00:00:02.120
(Transcribed by TurboScribe. Go Unlimited to remove this message.) Today's video is going to be very important

2
00:00:02.120 --> 00:00:03.780
because in this I will tell you what

3
00:00:03.780 --> 00:00:06.420
is a workbook, what is a sheet, what

4
00:00:06.420 --> 00:00:08.200
is a cell, and why it is very

5
00:00:08.200 --> 00:00:09.880
important for you to understand all this.

6
00:00:11.060 --> 00:00:12.380
So what I did here is I closed

7
00:00:12.380 --> 00:00:13.940
Excel, as you can see.

8
00:00:14.460 --> 00:00:17.000
And here I will quickly fire Excel again

9
00:00:17.000 --> 00:00:17.840
by typing excel.

10
00:00:18.420 --> 00:00:20.860
And when I click on it, I will

11
00:00:20.860 --> 00:00:22.020
get to see a screen like this.

12
00:00:22.700 --> 00:00:25.500
Here you see, it is writing blank workbook.

13
00:00:25.660 --> 00:00:26.840
What does workbook mean?

14
00:00:26.840 --> 00:00:28.600
As soon as I click on it, I

15
00:00:28.600 --> 00:00:29.320
get to see this.

16
00:00:29.320 --> 00:00:33.140
This is a workbook which has sheet and

17
00:00:33.140 --> 00:00:33.420
cell.

18
00:00:33.900 --> 00:00:34.260
What is sheet?

19
00:00:35.000 --> 00:00:36.720
Here you see, sheet 1 is written below.

20
00:00:37.260 --> 00:00:39.980
If I click on this plus below, then

21
00:00:39.980 --> 00:00:41.580
I can make a new sheet, sheet 2.

22
00:00:42.120 --> 00:00:43.420
Let's say I have sheet 1 here.

23
00:00:43.420 --> 00:00:44.040
I clicked here.

24
00:00:44.160 --> 00:00:45.400
You see, it is selected as sheet 1.

25
00:00:45.540 --> 00:00:47.900
I put data in it.

26
00:00:48.040 --> 00:00:49.560
Let's say I said serial number here.

27
00:00:49.640 --> 00:00:50.780
I wrote SNO here.

28
00:00:50.920 --> 00:00:52.380
I wrote 1 here.

29
00:00:52.440 --> 00:00:54.300
And I wrote title here.

30
00:00:54.300 --> 00:00:58.760
And I wrote, let's say, my book here.

31
00:00:58.900 --> 00:01:01.560
And I wrote remarks here.

32
00:01:01.980 --> 00:01:03.379
And let's capitalise the title key as well.

33
00:01:05.080 --> 00:01:05.760
Something like this.

34
00:01:05.980 --> 00:01:07.620
And I wrote nice book in remarks.

35
00:01:08.040 --> 00:01:09.720
So you can see here that I have

36
00:01:09.720 --> 00:01:10.660
put this data here.

37
00:01:11.440 --> 00:01:12.720
Now if I go to sheet 2, then

38
00:01:12.720 --> 00:01:13.560
this data has gone.

39
00:01:13.960 --> 00:01:14.840
Why did this happen?

40
00:01:15.500 --> 00:01:17.120
Why did this data go by clicking on

41
00:01:17.120 --> 00:01:17.220
sheet 2?

42
00:01:17.940 --> 00:01:19.220
The reason for this is that the data

43
00:01:19.220 --> 00:01:23.360
in sheet 1 is independent of the data

44
00:01:23.360 --> 00:01:24.200
in sheet 2.

45
00:01:24.780 --> 00:01:27.740
That is, there can be multiple sheets in

46
00:01:27.740 --> 00:01:29.620
a workbook in which multiple data can be

47
00:01:29.620 --> 00:01:29.720
there.

48
00:01:29.980 --> 00:01:31.280
But why was Excel made like this?

49
00:01:32.080 --> 00:01:34.860
Excel was made like this because it is

50
00:01:34.860 --> 00:01:37.600
possible that you want different sheets in a

51
00:01:37.600 --> 00:01:37.700
workbook.

52
00:01:38.180 --> 00:01:39.960
Let's say you are making a year planner.

53
00:01:40.740 --> 00:01:41.800
Let's say you are making a planner for

54
00:01:41.800 --> 00:01:44.080
your year 2026 or year 2027.

55
00:01:45.040 --> 00:01:47.380
That I will plan my year 2027 or

56
00:01:47.380 --> 00:01:48.680
year 2026 like this.

57
00:01:48.960 --> 00:01:49.560
So what will you do?

58
00:01:50.140 --> 00:01:50.640
You will give the name of one sheet

59
00:01:50.640 --> 00:01:51.580
as January.

60
00:01:52.120 --> 00:01:52.800
One as February.

61
00:01:52.800 --> 00:01:55.160
Plus you will make a new sheet.

62
00:01:55.440 --> 00:01:56.120
You will say March.

63
00:01:56.960 --> 00:01:58.920
Apple made all the way till December.

64
00:01:59.660 --> 00:02:01.480
But this is sheet 1, sheet 2, sheet

65
00:02:01.480 --> 00:02:01.700
3.

66
00:02:01.980 --> 00:02:03.820
This is not even January, February, March.

67
00:02:04.500 --> 00:02:06.980
How do I make it January, February, March?

68
00:02:07.500 --> 00:02:08.440
It's a good question.

69
00:02:09.000 --> 00:02:13.380
And this is where our sheet renaming feature

70
00:02:13.380 --> 00:02:13.480
comes in handy.

71
00:02:13.880 --> 00:02:15.540
That is, you can rename any sheet.

72
00:02:16.500 --> 00:02:18.520
Before I teach you how to rename, I

73
00:02:18.520 --> 00:02:20.000
want to show you that the data in

74
00:02:20.000 --> 00:02:22.280
sheet 1 is independent of the data in

75
00:02:22.280 --> 00:02:22.680
sheet 2.

76
00:02:22.680 --> 00:02:23.960
For example, if I click on sheet 2.

77
00:02:24.680 --> 00:02:26.840
And here let's say I write name.

78
00:02:27.340 --> 00:02:29.180
And here I write language.

79
00:02:29.520 --> 00:02:31.660
And here I write employee ID.

80
00:02:32.300 --> 00:02:34.820
And here let's say I write Rohan.

81
00:02:35.460 --> 00:02:36.360
And here I write language.

82
00:02:38.160 --> 00:02:38.680
Python.

83
00:02:38.900 --> 00:02:39.380
Python.

84
00:02:39.540 --> 00:02:40.660
This is a programming language by the way.

85
00:02:41.600 --> 00:02:42.120
1101.

86
00:02:42.620 --> 00:02:45.780
And here let's say I write Shubham.

87
00:02:45.900 --> 00:02:47.900
And here I write JavaScript.

88
00:02:48.260 --> 00:02:49.960
And here let's say employee ID is 1102.

89
00:02:50.520 --> 00:02:51.740
And I can populate this data more.

90
00:02:51.740 --> 00:02:54.140
But now I have only put this much

91
00:02:54.140 --> 00:02:54.780
data here.

92
00:02:54.820 --> 00:02:57.160
In Excel, most of the time, we put

93
00:02:57.160 --> 00:02:57.840
tabular data.

94
00:02:58.200 --> 00:02:58.760
That is, we put tables.

95
00:02:59.120 --> 00:03:00.460
In which there are column labels.

96
00:03:01.500 --> 00:03:03.800
And the row defines the data.

97
00:03:04.340 --> 00:03:07.120
That is, new data is inserted as rows.

98
00:03:07.720 --> 00:03:08.260
Now this is not necessary.

99
00:03:08.480 --> 00:03:09.560
But in most of the cases, this is

100
00:03:09.560 --> 00:03:09.780
what happens.

101
00:03:09.920 --> 00:03:12.520
If I click on sheet 1, I can

102
00:03:12.520 --> 00:03:13.000
see other data.

103
00:03:13.340 --> 00:03:14.320
If I click on sheet 2, I can

104
00:03:14.320 --> 00:03:14.740
see other data.

105
00:03:15.040 --> 00:03:16.260
If I click on sheet 3, it is

106
00:03:16.260 --> 00:03:16.580
empty.

107
00:03:16.920 --> 00:03:18.660
And I can make as many sheets as

108
00:03:18.660 --> 00:03:18.760
I want.

109
00:03:19.460 --> 00:03:21.220
Let's say I manage different companies.

110
00:03:22.500 --> 00:03:24.920
Let's say I manage my book company.

111
00:03:25.520 --> 00:03:28.200
Let's say its name is Harry Books Pvt.

112
00:03:28.220 --> 00:03:28.660
Ltd.

113
00:03:29.540 --> 00:03:31.160
So I can keep a sheet with this

114
00:03:31.160 --> 00:03:31.640
name.

115
00:03:32.600 --> 00:03:34.020
Or I make it LTD.

116
00:03:34.160 --> 00:03:35.280
Let's say this is Harry Books Ltd.

117
00:03:37.120 --> 00:03:39.480
And let's say my company's name is Harry

118
00:03:39.480 --> 00:03:40.820
Software Ltd.

119
00:03:40.940 --> 00:03:42.800
Harry Software Ltd.

120
00:03:43.220 --> 00:03:44.760
Okay, now I can write the name of

121
00:03:44.760 --> 00:03:45.080
this sheet in this way.

122
00:03:45.900 --> 00:03:47.320
And let me correct the spelling of Harry.

123
00:03:47.320 --> 00:03:48.900
How am I able to rename it?

124
00:03:49.460 --> 00:03:50.800
To rename it, you just have to double

125
00:03:50.800 --> 00:03:51.000
click.

126
00:03:51.620 --> 00:03:54.260
And then using the arrow key, you can

127
00:03:54.260 --> 00:03:54.920
type here.

128
00:03:54.980 --> 00:03:57.400
And then by hitting enter, you can finalise

129
00:03:57.400 --> 00:03:57.500
the name.

130
00:03:58.040 --> 00:03:59.240
It's as simple as that.

131
00:03:59.900 --> 00:04:00.200
Let's say I want to keep the name

132
00:04:00.200 --> 00:04:00.800
of sheet 3.

133
00:04:02.400 --> 00:04:05.060
Let's say I want to put all the

134
00:04:05.060 --> 00:04:06.020
basic information in it.

135
00:04:06.500 --> 00:04:08.060
So I will give it the name Home.

136
00:04:09.500 --> 00:04:11.280
Now it would have been great if Home

137
00:04:11.280 --> 00:04:11.840
was in the starting.

138
00:04:12.500 --> 00:04:14.720
Because I want my Home sheet to come

139
00:04:14.720 --> 00:04:16.459
in the starting as first sheet.

140
00:04:16.459 --> 00:04:18.720
And the rest of the sheets like Harry

141
00:04:18.720 --> 00:04:19.440
Books Ltd.

142
00:04:19.899 --> 00:04:21.040
Harry Software Ltd.

143
00:04:21.440 --> 00:04:22.140
All this comes later.

144
00:04:22.800 --> 00:04:24.520
What you simply do is by holding it

145
00:04:24.520 --> 00:04:24.940
with the mouse.

146
00:04:25.180 --> 00:04:26.040
That is, by pressing the click of your

147
00:04:26.040 --> 00:04:26.620
mouse.

148
00:04:27.940 --> 00:04:30.300
You can shift it in this way.

149
00:04:30.860 --> 00:04:32.380
Now do you see a triangle up here?

150
00:04:32.700 --> 00:04:33.780
Ultra Triangular Triangle.

151
00:04:33.980 --> 00:04:35.780
It will insert this sheet here.

152
00:04:35.980 --> 00:04:37.260
So if I bring it here and leave

153
00:04:37.260 --> 00:04:37.920
it, it will come in the starting.

154
00:04:38.260 --> 00:04:39.560
If I hold it and leave it here,

155
00:04:39.600 --> 00:04:40.220
it will come in the centre.

156
00:04:40.600 --> 00:04:41.900
If I hold it and leave it here,

157
00:04:41.900 --> 00:04:42.600
it will come here.

158
00:04:43.080 --> 00:04:44.420
And I can shift any sheet anywhere.

159
00:04:45.460 --> 00:04:47.520
Let's say I want Harry Software Ltd.

160
00:04:47.640 --> 00:04:49.520
to come first from Harry Books Ltd.

161
00:04:50.340 --> 00:04:51.900
So I can hold it like this and

162
00:04:51.900 --> 00:04:52.920
do it here.

163
00:04:52.980 --> 00:04:54.040
As simple as that.

164
00:04:54.680 --> 00:04:57.220
So yeah, that was about sheets.

165
00:04:57.660 --> 00:04:58.920
And how you can make sheets.

166
00:04:59.680 --> 00:05:02.180
Keep clicking on the plus and keep getting

167
00:05:02.180 --> 00:05:02.280
the sheets.

168
00:05:02.960 --> 00:05:04.240
And you can also delete a sheet.

169
00:05:04.680 --> 00:05:05.760
Let's say I made sheet 4 by mistake.

170
00:05:06.540 --> 00:05:07.400
What should I do now?

171
00:05:07.860 --> 00:05:08.340
Right click.

172
00:05:08.860 --> 00:05:09.340
Click on delete.

173
00:05:10.040 --> 00:05:10.920
And as soon as you click on delete,

174
00:05:11.980 --> 00:05:12.820
this sheet will be deleted.

175
00:05:13.880 --> 00:05:16.080
Now let's say that I want the colour

176
00:05:16.080 --> 00:05:16.440
of Home to be different.

177
00:05:16.760 --> 00:05:17.760
The colour of Harry Software Ltd.

178
00:05:17.840 --> 00:05:18.240
to be different.

179
00:05:18.620 --> 00:05:19.740
And the colour of Harry Books Ltd.

180
00:05:19.800 --> 00:05:20.260
to be different.

181
00:05:21.100 --> 00:05:22.400

00:10:08.400 --> 00:10:09.340

352
00:10:18.400 --> 00:10:19.440
In Excel.

00:10:19.980 --> 00:10:21.380
Every cell has an address.

354
00:10:21.980 --> 00:10:24.100
Means you can uniquely call every cell.

355
00:10:24.380 --> 00:10:24.860

357

00:10:31.060 --> 00:10:32.300

,D.

56.

00:11:59.860 --> 00:12:00.420
Excel cell.

411
00:12:01.120 --> 00:12:01.960
But at this point.

412
00:12:02.560 --> 00:12:04.100
We are seeing that.

413
00:12:04.220 --> 00:12:05.560
Changing in one sheet does not affect the

414

other sheet.

415
00:12:06.680 --> 00:12:08.320
Means sheets are independent.

416
00:12:08.840 --> 00:12:09.500
Of each other.

417
00:12:09.700 --> 00:12:11.380
Here you can see autosave is written.

418
00:12:11.580 --> 00:12:12.920
If you click here.

419
00:12:13.360 --> 00:12:14.840
Your autosave will be on.

420
00:12:15.380 --> 00:12:16.340
And it is telling you.

421
00:12:16.820 --> 00:12:18.920
If you want to turn autosave on.

422
00:12:19.440 --> 00:12:20.640
You have to upload the file in OneDrive.

423
00:12:20.640 --> 00:12:22.320
You have to upload the file in OneDrive.

424
00:12:22.880 --> 00:12:24.320
And autosave is turned on.

425
00:12:24.320 --> 00:12:27.060
When you save your file in OneDrive.

426
00:12:27.720 --> 00:12:29.320
OneDrive is a product of Microsoft.

427
00:12:29.540 --> 00:12:30.400
And it is a good idea.

428
00:12:30.500 --> 00:12:31.260
That you instal OneDrive.

429
00:12:31.800 --> 00:12:32.420
If you use Windows.

430
00:12:32.900 --> 00:12:34.000
OneDrive will be installed.

431
00:12:34.380 --> 00:12:35.660
But if you use Macbook.

432
00:12:36.260 --> 00:12:38.380
You get 5 GB free storage.

433
00:12:39.500 --> 00:12:40.740
And to save such files.

434
00:12:41.260 --> 00:12:43.120
You can use free 5 GB storage.

435
00:12:43.900 --> 00:12:44.920
I think it is very handy.

436
00:12:45.080 --> 00:12:47.800
Right now I have OneDrive's Microsoft 365 subscription.

437
00:12:47.800 --> 00:12:49.360
But back in the days when I was

438
00:12:49.360 --> 00:12:49.540
in college.

439
00:12:49.780 --> 00:12:50.540
I didn't have subscription.

440
00:12:50.980 --> 00:12:52.000
Then I used to use this 5 GB

441
00:12:52.000 --> 00:12:53.040
storage.

442
00:12:53.900 --> 00:12:55.000
For my documents.

443
00:12:55.100 --> 00:12:56.100
Because they are very important things.

444
00:12:56.380 --> 00:12:57.260
And their size is very less.

445
00:12:57.500 --> 00:12:59.260
For example, when I went to Singapore.

446
00:12:59.580 --> 00:13:00.840
I got a certificate from NUS.

447
00:13:00.940 --> 00:13:01.980
Which was only 200 KB.

448
00:13:02.680 --> 00:13:05.920
This 200 KB certificate was very important for

449
00:13:05.920 --> 00:13:06.020
me.

450
00:13:06.120 --> 00:13:07.100
I saved it in OneDrive.

451
00:13:08.120 --> 00:13:09.740
So you guys save such files.

452
00:13:09.840 --> 00:13:10.400
Which are also important.

453
00:13:12.460 --> 00:13:13.760
If you lose your computer.

454
00:13:13.760 --> 00:13:15.140
Your computer gets lost.

455
00:13:15.420 --> 00:13:17.020
Your computer gets formatted.

456
00:13:17.020 --> 00:13:18.320
And it happens in years.

457
00:13:18.540 --> 00:13:20.040
You won't use the same laptop after 10

458
00:13:20.040 --> 00:13:20.220
years.

459
00:13:20.340 --> 00:13:21.100
There is a good chance you will use

460
00:13:21.100 --> 00:13:21.580
another laptop.

461
00:13:22.420 --> 00:13:24.020
But if you have OneDrive.

462
00:13:24.200 --> 00:13:25.700
And that is the same phone number.

463
00:13:25.800 --> 00:13:28.680
You can easily recover your files.

464
00:13:28.800 --> 00:13:30.160
So if your file is saved in OneDrive.

465
00:13:30.760 --> 00:13:32.300
Then you click on the auto save option.

466
00:13:33.160 --> 00:13:34.160
Then it will be turned on.

00:13:34.660 --> 00:13:36.280
One more important thing I want to tell

468
00:13:36.280 --> 00:13:36.380

And what can you do to fix it?

58
00:01:57.230 --> 00:01:57.750
Click on the cell.

59
00:01:58.470 --> 00:02:00.230
Then you can edit it here.

60
00:02:00.470 --> 00:02:03.250
So ADVANCD is there.

61
00:02:03.270 --> 00:02:04.690
So I will click here and hit enter.

62
00:02:04.970 --> 00:02:06.830
So my value has been corrected here.

63
00:02:06.930 --> 00:02:07.550
Now I will click on this cell.

64
00:02:08.250 --> 00:02:10.850
And after clicking on this cell, I can

65
00:02:10.850 --> 00:02:11.130

66
00:02:11.310 --> 00:02:15.250
I will write Advanced Features of Excel.

67
00:02:15.650 --> 00:02:17.410
Advanced Features of Excel.

68
00:02:17.850 --> 00:02:19.210
One more thing I want to tell you

69
00:02:19.210 --> 00:02:22.530
is that The width of your column is

70
00:02:22.530 --> 00:02:24.170
similar to all the columns.

71
00:02:24.390 --> 00:02:25.530
In fact, it is exactly the same width.

72
00:02:26.510 --> 00:02:28.190
If I want this column to take a

73
00:02:28.190 --> 00:02:28.750
little more width.

74
00:02:28.930 --> 00:02:30.150
So that I can see its data well.

75
00:02:30.510 --> 00:02:31.770
And this column also takes a little more

76
00:02:31.770 --> 00:02:31.990
width.

77
00:02:32.250 --> 00:02:32.810
Take it up to here.

78
00:02:33.210 --> 00:02:35.330
So I can make its width smaller and

79
00:02:35.330 --> 00:02:35.610

80
00:02:36.210 --> 00:02:37.810
And here you can see that my data

81
00:02:37.810 --> 00:02:41.430
It will adapt the width of this column

82
00:02:41.430 --> 00:02:42.430

83
00:02:43.350 --> 00:02:47.490

84
00:02:47.490 --> 00:02:48.390

85
00:02:48.390 --> 00:02:50.390

00:02:51.090 --> 00:02:53.150

87

88
00:02:54.350 --> 00:02:56.710

89
00:02:56.710 --> 00:02:57.450

00:02:58.010 --> 00:03:00.390

91

93
00:03:03.330 --> 00:03:05.470
It simply means that whatever changes you have

94
00:03:05.470 --> 00:03:05.950
made here.

95
00:03:06.470 --> 00:03:08.450

96
00:03:08.870 --> 00:03:11.050
Means you will lose all the changes that

97
00:03:11.050 --> 00:03:11.630

98
00:03:11.710 --> 00:03:15.230
For example, I have written Basics of Excel

99
00:03:15.230 --> 00:03:15.350

100
00:03:15.350 --> 00:03:16.410

101
00:03:16.410 --> 00:03:17.930
All these things that I have written, whatever

102
00:03:17.930 --> 00:03:18.730

103
00:03:19.110 --> 00:03:19.790
They will all go.

104
00:03:20.170 --> 00:03:21.990
And my workbook will be reset.

105
00:03:22.790 --> 00:03:24.370
In previous saved configuration.

106
00:03:24.530 --> 00:03:26.690
That is, when I saved it earlier, it

107
00:03:26.690 --> 00:03:27.550
will be the same as it was seen.

108
00:03:27.790 --> 00:03:29.970
To avoid this, you click on save here.

109
00:03:30.570 --> 00:03:32.250
The background of green color on save is

110
00:03:32.250 --> 00:03:35.130
because Excel really wants you to save.

111
00:03:35.250 --> 00:03:36.970
Because if you have changed, then there is

112
00:03:36.970 --> 00:03:37.750
a good chance you will want to save.

113
00:03:38.490 --> 00:03:40.410
So this green color button shows you that

114
00:03:40.410 --> 00:03:41.390
by mistake you do not click on save.

115
00:03:41.810 --> 00:03:42.450
You do it on save.

116
00:03:43.190 --> 00:03:43.850
It is highlighting it.

117
00:03:43.850 --> 00:03:45.590
Excel is basically telling you.

118
00:03:46.370 --> 00:03:48.210
Probably this is something you want to do

119
00:03:48.210 --> 00:03:48.690
at this point.

120
00:03:48.890 --> 00:03:49.290
You want to save.

121
00:03:49.770 --> 00:03:50.390
Click on it.

122
00:03:50.470 --> 00:03:51.090

123
00:03:51.310 --> 00:03:52.270
It is making it easy for you to

124
00:03:52.270 --> 00:03:52.670

125
00:03:53.110 --> 00:03:54.670
And I will click on save here.

00:03:54.950 --> 00:03:56.550
And here you see the introductory sheet.

00:03:56.730 --> 00:03:58.830
If I double click on Excel SX.

00:03:59.390 --> 00:04:00.890

00:04:01.650 --> 00:04:03.090
Let's say I put some data here.

00:04:03.350 --> 00:04:04.910
Let's say this is realistic data.

131
00:04:05.090 --> 00:04:06.310
I really don't want to waste a lot

132
00:04:06.310 --> 00:04:06.650

133
00:04:07.090 --> 00:04:09.230

00:04:09.310 --> 00:04:10.930

00:04:17.570 --> 00:04:19.130
So now if I open this file, then

00:04:19.130 --> 00:04:20.430
you see here.

00:04:20.450 --> 00:04:22.230
My book, Excel basics, Excel advanced.

142
00:04:22.370 --> 00:04:23.550
That means whatever data I added.

143
00:04:23.930 --> 00:04:25.410
You will not get it.

144
00:04:25.490 --> 00:04:26.630
To see inside this workbook.

145
00:04:27.530 --> 00:04:28.470
Whenever you want to open a workbook.

146
00:04:29.290 --> 00:04:31.190
Then you can double click here and open

147
00:04:31.190 --> 00:04:31.290

148
00:04:31.650 --> 00:04:34.090
But sometimes what happens is that if it

00:04:34.090 --> 00:04:35.350

00:04:35.430 --> 00:04:36.070
So what will you do?

151
00:04:36.190 --> 00:04:36.830
You will open Excel first.

00:04:37.410 --> 00:04:38.250

153
00:04:38.610 --> 00:04:39.230

154

155
00:04:40.710 --> 00:04:41.790
But it happens.

00:04:44.290 --> 00:04:45.410

00:04:46.030 --> 00:04:46.970
By the way, it will show you.

163
00:04:50.890 --> 00:04:51.290

164
00:04:51.790 --> 00:04:53.190

165
00:04:53.750 --> 00:04:55.110

166
00:04:55.350 --> 00:04:56.290

00:04:56.550 --> 00:04:57.950
But if your file is somewhere else.

00:04:58.090 --> 00:04:58.890

00:05:00.030 --> 00:05:00.630
And it will open.

00:05:01.070 --> 00:05:02.950
This is one way to open a file.

The most basic way to open an Excel

00:05:07.490 --> 00:05:08.450
But this is another way.

177
00:05:08.450 --> 00:05:09.750

By pressing CTRL O.

179
00:05:11.030 --> 00:05:11.950
As soon as you press CTRL O.

180
00:05:12.610 --> 00:05:13.910
You can open any file.

181
00:05:14.350 --> 00:05:14.790
For example.

00:05:14.930 --> 00:05:16.470

00:05:17.630 --> 00:05:18.490
Then you see.

00:05:18.710 --> 00:05:20.150

00:05:20.450 --> 00:05:22.510

00:05:22.830 --> 00:05:24.910
And here is sampledata.xlsx open.

00:05:25.630 --> 00:05:27.110
Whenever you open a new Excel file.

00:05:27.850 --> 00:05:29.110
Here it says protected view.

189
00:05:29.270 --> 00:05:30.130
It says be careful.

190
00:05:30.430 --> 00:05:32.310
Files from the internet can contain viruses.

191
00:05:32.510 --> 00:05:33.430
Unless you need to edit.

192
00:05:33.610 --> 00:05:35.170
It is safer to stay in protected mode.

193
00:05:35.250 --> 00:05:36.590
So protected mode basically means.

194
00:05:36.590 --> 00:05:38.310
That you can see the data.

195
00:05:38.790 --> 00:05:40.470
You can do CTRL C here.

196
00:05:40.590 --> 00:05:41.690
You can do CTRL V here.

197
00:05:41.950 --> 00:05:42.870
Copy paste will also work.

198
00:05:43.570 --> 00:05:45.730
But you can't make changes in this file.

199
00:05:46.450 --> 00:05:46.990
And Excel says.

200
00:05:47.130 --> 00:05:48.890
If you just want to see the data.

201
00:05:49.150 --> 00:05:49.810
If you want to see the data.

202
00:05:50.470 --> 00:05:50.770
You want to copy.

203
00:05:51.310 --> 00:05:53.030
You want to do some small manipulations.

204
00:05:53.630 --> 00:05:54.710
Like you want to select them.

205
00:05:55.490 --> 00:05:56.170
What is count?

206
00:05:56.530 --> 00:05:56.710
What is sum?

207
00:05:57.050 --> 00:05:57.410
What is average?

208
00:05:57.630 --> 00:05:58.590
Which you can see here.

209
00:05:58.790 --> 00:05:59.550
We will talk about this later.

210
00:06:00.370 --> 00:06:01.630
But what does protected mode mean?

211
00:06:02.170 --> 00:06:04.050
That your file.

212
00:06:04.050 --> 00:06:06.190
Can't be edited.

213
00:06:06.890 --> 00:06:08.290
You are just looking at it.

214
00:06:08.450 --> 00:06:09.410
You are in view only mode.

215
00:06:09.710 --> 00:06:10.550
You are not enabling editing.

216
00:06:10.930 --> 00:06:11.490
You are not changing this file.

217
00:06:11.990 --> 00:06:12.130
Okay.

218
00:06:12.570 --> 00:06:13.570
I will close this file for now.

219
00:06:14.250 --> 00:06:15.370
And he didn't say anything to me.

220
00:06:15.530 --> 00:06:16.230
He didn't show me any box.

221
00:06:16.430 --> 00:06:17.770
Because I didn't change anything in it.

222
00:06:18.130 --> 00:06:19.330
If I had changed anything in it.

223
00:06:19.890 --> 00:06:20.530
Then he would have asked me.

224
00:06:20.670 --> 00:06:22.150
Do you want to save the changes?

225
00:06:22.310 --> 00:06:22.430
Okay.

226
00:06:22.790 --> 00:06:23.630
But he didn't do that.

227
00:06:24.230 --> 00:06:24.510
So yeah.

00:06:25.150 --> 00:06:26.470
We can do something like this.

229
00:06:26.770 --> 00:06:27.210
And.

230
00:06:27.470 --> 00:06:28.650
Now once you have made such files.

231
00:06:28.950 --> 00:06:29.890
You can also distribute them.

232
00:06:30.530 --> 00:06:31.410
You can distribute.

233
00:06:31.890 --> 00:06:32.350
With the help of OneDrive.

234
00:06:33.050 --> 00:06:33.490
For example.

235
00:06:33.490 --> 00:06:34.970
If your folder is in OneDrive.

236
00:06:35.510 --> 00:06:36.350
Then you right click.

237
00:06:36.970 --> 00:06:37.730
And there is a share option.

238
00:06:38.450 --> 00:06:39.650
There is a share option on the side

239
00:06:39.650 --> 00:06:39.750
of OneDrive.

240
00:06:40.050 --> 00:06:41.250
To demonstrate this.

241
00:06:41.350 --> 00:06:43.050
I will copy this file and show you.

242
00:06:43.130 --> 00:06:44.230
In my OneDrive folder.

243
00:06:44.430 --> 00:06:45.910
So my folder is in OneDrive.

244
00:06:46.630 --> 00:06:47.590
And here you can see.

245
00:06:47.770 --> 00:06:49.850
I can right click and share it.

246
00:06:50.230 --> 00:06:52.030
I can go to OneDrive and share it.

247
00:06:52.090 --> 00:06:52.530
I can copy link.

248
00:06:52.970 --> 00:06:54.690
And I can view it online too.

249
00:06:55.370 --> 00:06:57.090
And I can send a link to my

250
00:06:57.090 --> 00:06:57.190
friend.

251
00:06:57.270 --> 00:06:58.670
So that he can also access it.

252
00:06:59.490 --> 00:07:00.130
So we will right click.

253
00:07:00.570 --> 00:07:01.110
We will go to OneDrive.

254
00:07:01.110 --> 00:07:02.490
We will click on copy link.

255
00:07:03.470 --> 00:07:05.550
And whatever link it will generate after this.

256
00:07:05.790 --> 00:07:06.450
On that you guys.

257
00:07:07.170 --> 00:07:08.530
There is basically an option of copy link

258
00:07:08.530 --> 00:07:08.730
here.

259
00:07:08.910 --> 00:07:09.770
If you click on it.

260
00:07:09.970 --> 00:07:11.650
Then your link will be copied.

261
00:07:12.050 --> 00:07:13.650
And after that you can share it.

262
00:07:14.070 --> 00:07:15.470
Through SMS.  Through WhatsApp.

263
00:07:15.730 --> 00:07:16.810
Through any medium.

264
00:07:16.950 --> 00:07:18.470
Basically you have to send this link.

265
00:07:19.290 --> 00:07:20.270
To your friends and family.

266
00:07:20.430 --> 00:07:21.210
And they will be able to access it.

267
00:07:21.910 --> 00:07:23.350
So this is a way to share files.

268
00:07:24.050 --> 00:07:25.050
But the easiest way is.

269
00:07:25.130 --> 00:07:25.230
You can send it by attaching it in

270
00:07:25.230 --> 00:07:25.590
an email.

271
00:07:27.010 --> 00:07:28.610
You can copy it in a pen drive.

272
00:07:28.610 --> 00:07:31.470
You can put it in a hard disk.

273
00:07:31.890 --> 00:07:32.810
Basically this is a file.

274
00:07:33.250 --> 00:07:34.570
You will give it to any person.

275
00:07:34.710 --> 00:07:35.570
He will be able to view its data.

276
00:07:36.290 --> 00:07:37.250
In exactly the same condition.

277
00:07:37.850 --> 00:07:39.570
Provided that he has Excel installed.

278
00:07:39.950 --> 00:07:41.570
Okay, a lot of people have a question.

279
00:07:41.670 --> 00:07:43.430
If I have made an Excel on Mac.

280
00:07:43.630 --> 00:07:44.410
So can I take it to Windows.

281
00:07:44.830 --> 00:07:45.670
And view it completely normally.

282
00:07:46.370 --> 00:07:47.570
The answer is yes you can.

283
00:07:47.790 --> 00:07:48.630
You can view it completely normally.

284
00:07:49.610 --> 00:07:51.270
Whether you have made that file on Excel

285
00:07:51.270 --> 00:07:51.830
on Mac.

286
00:07:52.330 --> 00:07:53.430
Whether you have made Excel on Windows.

287
00:07:54.270 --> 00:07:55.850
If .xlsx is the format.

288
00:07:56.450 --> 00:07:57.230
Then you can view it.

289
00:07:57.830 --> 00:07:59.470
No matter where you have made that file.

290
00:07:59.890 --> 00:08:01.970
So .xlsx is valid.

291
00:08:02.190 --> 00:08:03.150
If your file is in .xlsx format.

292
00:08:03.830 --> 00:08:05.210
This is not the name of any file.

293
00:08:05.370 --> 00:08:07.070
You have named it .xlsx. I am not

294
00:08:07.070 --> 00:08:07.790
talking about this.

295
00:08:08.230 --> 00:08:09.010
I am saying that if you.

296
00:08:09.030 --> 00:08:10.210
From any Excel version.

297
00:08:10.370 --> 00:08:10.970
In any PC.

298
00:08:11.210 --> 00:08:11.970
In any Windows.

299
00:08:12.170 --> 00:08:12.490
In Mac.

300
00:08:13.330 --> 00:08:13.830
I should say.

301
00:08:13.950 --> 00:08:14.870
In any operating system.

302
00:08:15.290 --> 00:08:15.890
If you.

303
00:08:16.490 --> 00:08:17.290
Save the file as it is.

304
00:08:17.970 --> 00:08:19.930
And you go to any other operating system.

305
00:08:20.710 --> 00:08:22.110
So in that other operating system.

306
00:08:22.370 --> 00:08:23.430
You will be able to view the file.

307
00:08:23.790 --> 00:08:24.570
That's the point.

308
00:08:24.570 --> 00:08:26.990
Now what are the file types of Excel.

309
00:08:27.030 --> 00:08:27.530
If you go to file.

310
00:08:27.910 --> 00:08:28.270
If you go to save as.

311
00:08:28.570 --> 00:08:29.930
You will get to see a lot of

312
00:08:29.930 --> 00:08:31.490
formats here.

313
00:08:31.990 --> 00:08:33.510
You have an Excel workbook.

314
00:08:33.650 --> 00:08:35.049
Which is in .xlsx format.

315
00:08:35.309 --> 00:08:36.070
You should use this 90% of the

316
00:08:36.070 --> 00:08:36.330
time.

317
00:08:37.730 --> 00:08:39.409
Then Excel Macro Enabled Workbook.

318
00:08:39.610 --> 00:08:40.510
Excel SM.

319
00:08:40.990 --> 00:08:42.330
Then Excel Binary Workbook.

320
00:08:42.690 --> 00:08:43.730
Then Excel S.

321
00:08:44.030 --> 00:08:45.690
Which is used for 97-2003.

322
00:08:45.830 --> 00:08:46.650
Old version of Excel.

323
00:08:47.450 --> 00:08:49.210
What happened from 2006 onwards.

324
00:08:49.490 --> 00:08:51.530
That Excel SX was introduced.

325
00:08:51.810 --> 00:08:52.710
And Excel SX.

326
00:08:52.710 --> 00:08:53.510
Started being used.

327
00:08:54.230 --> 00:08:55.590
As default format.

328
00:08:55.690 --> 00:08:57.290
So here are the old formats.

329
00:08:57.450 --> 00:08:58.110
There is XLT.

330
00:08:58.610 --> 00:09:00.210
Then there is tab delimited text file.

331
00:09:00.690 --> 00:09:03.070
We have a lot of formats here.

332
00:09:03.110 --> 00:09:03.930
And PDF is also there.

333
00:09:03.990 --> 00:09:05.110
You know what PDF is.

334
00:09:05.390 --> 00:09:06.590
But what we will do.

335
00:09:06.690 --> 00:09:07.370
We will use Excel SX.

336
00:09:08.410 --> 00:09:10.070
And most of you.

337
00:09:10.130 --> 00:09:10.890
You should use this format.

338
00:09:11.410 --> 00:09:12.390
In fact, if you don't know.

339
00:09:12.450 --> 00:09:13.590

00:09:13.910 --> 00:09:15.870

00:06:04.490 --> 00:06:05.130

00:06:05.410 --> 00:06:07.530
So line by line because python programme is

201
00:06:07.530 --> 00:06:07.990

00:06:09.250 --> 00:06:10.610

00:06:10.850 --> 00:06:11.990

204

00:06:14.510 --> 00:06:15.330

207
00:06:16.030 --> 00:06:18.530
Because python is an interpreted language.

208
00:06:18.770 --> 00:06:21.210
Means your code is processed line by line.

209
00:06:21.210 --> 00:06:23.290
First of all, line number 1 will be

00:06:23.290 --> 00:06:23.390

211

212
00:06:25.170 --> 00:06:26.630

213
00:06:26.790 --> 00:06:27.810
If line number 3 is done.

214
00:06:28.110 --> 00:06:29.070
And so on.

215
00:06:29.450 --> 00:06:30.890
So what happened here last time.

216
00:06:31.190 --> 00:06:33.010

00:06:52.610 --> 00:06:53.670

00:06:55.130 --> 00:06:56.190

00:10:02.430 --> 00:10:04.110

00:10:13.750 --> 00:10:15.370

00:10:17.490 --> 00:10:18.670

00:10:21.330 --> 00:10:23.630

00:10:24.270 --> 00:10:26.550

00:10:27.770 --> 00:10:29.690

345
00:10:29.690 --> 00:10:30.390

00:10:30.690 --> 00:10:31.490

00:10:32.150 --> 00:10:34.210

00:10:34.730 --> 00:10:35.590

00:10:36.290 --> 00:10:37.350

00:10:37.350 --> 00:10:37.550

00:10:40.270 --> 00:10:41.850

00:10:46.410 --> 00:10:47.310

00:10:48.790 --> 00:10:50.190

00:10:50.910 --> 00:10:52.330
Remove this particular package.

359
00:10:53.610 --> 00:10:55.030

00:10:55.030 --> 00:10:56.830

00:10:56.950 --> 00:10:57.710
